EAST BRANCH DELAWARE RIVER AT FISHS EDDY NY, USGS site 01421000 (USGS-01421000), stream in Delaware County, New York, draining 784 square miles. Daily means from 1912-11-19 to 2025-12-08, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
